// src/lib/submissions.ts
import { api } from "./api";

/**
 * createSubmission: posts to /submissions
 * body shape based on backend `submissions` model (see openapi)
 *
 * Minimal required fields:
 *  - content_type (string)
 *  - main_text (string)
 *  - contributor_id is handled server-side via auth
 *
 * Optional:
 *  - meaning, is_classical, author_slug, work_slug, chapter_slug,
 *    number_in_chapter, external_references (object), status, visibility
 */
export async function createSubmission(payload: Record<string, any>) {
  return await api("/submissions", { method: "POST", body: payload });
}

export async function listMySubmissions(params?: {
  status?: string;
  content_type?: string;
  offset?: number;
  limit?: number;
}) {
  const queryParams = new URLSearchParams();
  if (params?.status) queryParams.set("status", params.status);
  if (params?.content_type) queryParams.set("content_type", params.content_type);
  if (params?.offset !== undefined) queryParams.set("offset", String(params.offset));
  if (params?.limit !== undefined) queryParams.set("limit", String(params.limit));
  
  const queryString = queryParams.toString();
  return await api(`/submissions/me${queryString ? `?${queryString}` : ""}`);
}

// Authors / Works / Chapters helpers (use existing backend routes)
export async function listAuthors() {
  return await api("/authors");
}

export async function listWorksForAuthor(authorSlug: string) {
  return await api(`/authors/${encodeURIComponent(authorSlug)}/works`);
}

export async function listChaptersForWork(authorSlug: string, workSlug: string) {
  return await api(
    `/authors/${encodeURIComponent(authorSlug)}/works/${encodeURIComponent(workSlug)}/chapters`
  );
}

          {idioms.map((idiom) => (
            <li class="border-l-4 border-indigo-400 pl-4 hover:bg-slate-700/30 p-3 rounded transition-colors">
              <a href={`/idioms/${idiom.id}`} class="text-indigo-300 hover:text-indigo-200 font-medium text-lg block mb-1">
                {idiom.text_devanagari}
              </a>
              {idiom.text_roman && (
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 italic">{idiom.text_roman}</p>
              )}
              {idiom.meaning && (
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-400 mt-1">{idiom.meaning}</p>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>
    )}

    {!error && idioms.length === 0 && (
      <div class="text-center py-12 bg-slate-800/50 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
        <p class="text-slate-400">No idioms found.</p>
      </div>
    )}
  </div>
</BaseLayout>

import BaseLayout from "../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
export const prerender = false;
---

<BaseLayout title="Login · Awadhi New" noindex>
  <meta name="robots" content="noindex,nofollow" />
  
  <div class="max-w-md mx-auto mt-12 px-4">
    <h1 class="text-2xl font-serif mb-6">Login</h1>

    <form id="login-form" class="space-y-4" method="POST" action="javascript:void(0)" novalidate>
      <div>
        <label class="block text-sm mb-1">Email</label>
        <input
          type="email"
          name="email"
          required
          class="w-full border px-3 py-2 rounded bg-gray-800 text-white"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label class="block text-sm mb-1">Password</label>
        <input
          type="password"
          name="password"
          required
          class="w-full border px-3 py-2 rounded bg-gray-800 text-white"
        />
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        class="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-2 rounded hover:bg-blue-700 transition-colors"
      >
        Sign in
      </button>

      <p id="login-error" class="text-sm text-red-600 hidden"></p>

      <p class="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center">
        New here?
        <a href="/register" class="text-blue-500 hover:underline">Create an account</a>
      </p>
    </form>
  </div>

  <script is:inline>
    (async function() {
      // Wait for DOM
      if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        await new Promise(resolve => document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', resolve));
      }

      const form = document.getElementById("login-form");
      const errorEl = document.getElementById("login-error");

      if (!form || !errorEl) {
        console.error('[login] Form elements not found');
        return;
      }

      form.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        
        errorEl.classList.add("hidden");
        errorEl.textContent = "";

        const email = form.querySelector('[name="email"]').value.trim();
        const password = form.querySelector('[name="password"]').value;

        if (!email || !password) {
          errorEl.textContent = "Email and password are required";
          errorEl.classList.remove("hidden");
          return false;
        }

        try {
          const API_BASE = "http://localhost:8000";
          
          const payload = { email, password };
          console.log('[login] Sending request:', { email: payload.email, password: '***' });
          
          // Login request
          const res = await fetch(API_BASE + "/auth/login", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify(payload),
          });

          const data = await res.json();
          console.log('[login] Response status:', res.status, res.ok);

          if (!res.ok) {
            console.error('[login] Error response:', data);
            // Handle Pydantic validation errors (422)
            if (res.status === 422 && data.detail) {
              const errors = Array.isArray(data.detail) ? data.detail : [data.detail];
              const errorMsg = errors.map(e => {
                if (typeof e === 'object' && e.msg) {
                  return `${e.loc?.join('.') || 'Field'}: ${e.msg}`;
                }
                return typeof e === 'string' ? e : JSON.stringify(e);
              }).join(', ');
              throw new Error(errorMsg || "Validation failed");
            }
            throw new Error(data.detail || data.message || "Login failed");
          }
          
          // Store tokens
          localStorage.setItem("awadhi_access_token", data.access_token);
          localStorage.setItem("awadhi_refresh_token", data.refresh_token);
          
          // Fetch user info and cache it
          const meRes = await fetch(API_BASE + "/auth/me", {
            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${data.access_token}` },
          });
          
          if (meRes.ok) {
            const user = await meRes.json();
            localStorage.setItem("awadhi_user_cache", JSON.stringify(user));
          }

          window.location.href = "/";
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[login] Error:', err);
          errorEl.textContent = err.message || "Login failed";
          errorEl.classList.remove("hidden");
        }
        
        return false;
      });
    })();
  </script>
</BaseLayout>

    <h1 class="text-2xl font-serif mb-6">Create Account</h1>
    
    <form id="registerForm" class="space-y-4" method="POST" action="javascript:void(0)" novalidate>
      <div>
        <label class="block text-sm mb-1">Username</label>
        <input name="username" type="text" required class="w-full border px-3 py-2 rounded bg-gray-800 text-white" />
      </div>
      
      <div>
        <label class="block text-sm mb-1">Email</label>
        <input name="email" type="email" required class="w-full border px-3 py-2 rounded bg-gray-800 text-white" />
      </div>
      
      <div>
        <label class="block text-sm mb-1">Password</label>
        <input name="password" type="password" required class="w-full border px-3 py-2 rounded bg-gray-800 text-white" />
      </div>

      <button type="submit" class="w-full bg-blue-600 text-white py-2 rounded hover:bg-blue-700 transition-colors">Register</button>
      <p id="error" class="text-red-600 text-sm hidden"></p>
      
      <p class="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center">
        Already have an account?
        <a href="/login" class="text-blue-500 hover:underline">Sign in</a>
      </p>
    </form>
  </div>

  <script is:inline>
    (async function() {
      // Wait for DOM
      if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        await new Promise(resolve => document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', resolve));
      }

      const form = document.getElementById("registerForm");
      const errorEl = document.getElementById("error");

      if (!form || !errorEl) {
        console.error('[register] Form elements not found');
        return;
      }

      form.addEventListener("submit", async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        
        errorEl.classList.add("hidden");
        errorEl.textContent = "";

        const data = Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form).entries());

        try {
          const API_BASE = "http://localhost:8000";
          
          const res = await fetch(API_BASE + "/auth/register", {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify(data),
          });

          const json = await res.json();
          
          if (!res.ok) {
            throw new Error(json.detail || "Registration failed");
          }

          window.location.href = "/login";
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[register] Error:', err);
          errorEl.textContent = err.message || "Registration failed";
          errorEl.classList.remove("hidden");
        }
        
        return false;
      });
    })();
  </script>
</BaseLayout>

// src/pages/robots.txt.ts
export async function GET() {
  const robotsTxt = `
# Awadhi New - Robots.txt
# Allow crawling of public content, disallow private areas

User-agent: *

# Allow public content
Allow: /
Allow: /doha
Allow: /doha/*
Allow: /dictionary
Allow: /dictionary/*
Allow: /idioms
Allow: /idioms/*
Allow: /articles
Allow: /articles/*
Allow: /authors
Allow: /search
Allow: /about

# Disallow private/user areas
Disallow: /login
Disallow: /register
Disallow: /dashboard
Disallow: /dashboard/*
Disallow: /me
Disallow: /me/*
Disallow: /submit
Disallow: /submissions
Disallow: /submissions/*

# Disallow admin/moderation
Disallow: /admin
Disallow: /admin/*
Disallow: /moderation
Disallow: /moderation/*

# Disallow API endpoints (if exposed)
Disallow: /api
Disallow: /api/*

# Sitemap
Sitemap: https://awadhi.new/sitemap.xml
`.trim();

  return new Response(robotsTxt, {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
    },
  });
}


=======================================================
src\pages\search.astro

---
export const prerender = false;

import BaseLayout from "../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import { api } from "../lib/api";

const url = new URL(Astro.request.url);
const q = url.searchParams.get("q");
const contentFilter = url.searchParams.get("type") || "all";
const sortBy = url.searchParams.get("sort") || "relevance";
const authorFilter = url.searchParams.get("author") || "";
const workFilter = url.searchParams.get("work") || "";

let dohas = [];
let dictionary = [];
let idioms = [];
let articles = [];
let errors = [];

if (q) {
  console.log("[SEARCH SSR] Query:", q);

  // Build search URLs with all filters
  const buildSearchUrl = (endpoint: string) => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    params.set("q", q);
    if (authorFilter) params.set("author", authorFilter);
    if (workFilter) params.set("work", workFilter);
    if (sortBy !== "relevance") params.set("sort", sortBy);
    params.set("limit", "20");
    return `${endpoint}?${params.toString()}`;
  };

  // Execute all searches in parallel for better performance
  const [dohaRes, dictRes, idiomRes, articleRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
    api(buildSearchUrl("/search")),
    api(`/dictionary?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&limit=20`),
    api(`/idioms?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&limit=20`),
    api(`/articles?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&limit=20`)
  ]);

  // Process doha results (returns {results: [...]} wrapper)
  if (dohaRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
    dohas = dohaRes.value?.results ?? dohaRes.value ?? [];
    console.log("[SEARCH SSR] Doha results:", dohas.length, dohas);
  } else {
    console.error("[SEARCH SSR] Doha search failed", dohaRes.reason);
    errors.push("Doha search failed");
  }

  // Process dictionary results (returns flat array)
  if (dictRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
    dictionary = Array.isArray(dictRes.value) ? dictRes.value : [];
    console.log("[SEARCH SSR] Dictionary results:", dictionary.length, dictionary);
  } else {
    console.error("[SEARCH SSR] Dictionary search failed", dictRes.reason);
    errors.push("Dictionary search failed");
  }

  // Process idiom results (returns flat array)
  if (idiomRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
    idioms = Array.isArray(idiomRes.value) ? idiomRes.value : [];
    console.log("[SEARCH SSR] Idiom results:", idioms.length, idioms);
  } else {
    console.error("[SEARCH SSR] Idiom search failed", idiomRes.reason);
    errors.push("Idiom search failed");
  }

  // Process article results (returns flat array)
  if (articleRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
    articles = Array.isArray(articleRes.value) ? articleRes.value : [];
    console.log("[SEARCH SSR] Article results:", articles.length, articles);
  } else {
    console.error("[SEARCH SSR] Article search failed", articleRes.reason);
    errors.push("Article search failed");
  }
}

const totalResults = dohas.length + dictionary.length + idioms.length + articles.length;
---

<BaseLayout title={q ? `Search: ${q}` : "Search"}>
  <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">
    <h1 class="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-serif font-bold mb-8 text-center bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 via-blue-400 to-indigo-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
      Search Awadhi Corpus
    </h1>

    <form method="get" action="/search" class="mb-10">
      <div class="flex gap-3 mb-4">
        <input
          name="q"
          value={q ?? ""}
          placeholder="Search across Doha, Dictionary, Idioms, and Articles..."
          class="flex-1 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-600 text-slate-200 px-4 py-3 rounded-lg focus:border-cyan-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-cyan-400/50 transition-all"
          required
        />
        <button class="px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 to-blue-500 hover:from-cyan-400 hover:to-blue-400 text-white font-semibold rounded-lg shadow-lg transition-all">
          Search
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- Advanced filters: Author & Work (for Doha) -->
      {q && (
        <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 mb-4">
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1.5">Filter by Author (optional)</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              name="author"
              value={authorFilter}
              placeholder="e.g., kabir, tulsidas"
              class="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-600 text-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded-lg focus:border-cyan-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-400/50 transition-all"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1.5">Filter by Work (optional)</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              name="work"
              value={workFilter}
              placeholder="e.g., ramcharitmanas"
              class="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-600 text-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm rounded-lg focus:border-cyan-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-400/50 transition-all"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      
      {q && (
        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <span class="text-sm text-slate-400">Filter:</span>
          <input type="hidden" name="q" value={q} />
          {authorFilter && <input type="hidden" name="author" value={authorFilter} />}
          {workFilter && <input type="hidden" name="work" value={workFilter} />}
          
          <!-- Active filter chips with remove option -->
          {authorFilter && (
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm rounded-full bg-cyan-900/50 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-700">
              <span>Author: {authorFilter}</span>
              <a href={`/search?q=${q}${workFilter ? `&work=${workFilter}` : ''}${contentFilter !== 'all' ? `&type=${contentFilter}` : ''}${sortBy !== 'relevance' ? `&sort=${sortBy}` : ''}`} 
                 class="text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-200 ml-1"
                 aria-label="Remove author filter">×</a>
            </div>
          )}
          {workFilter && (
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm rounded-full bg-blue-900/50 text-blue-300 border border-blue-700">
              <span>Work: {workFilter}</span>
              <a href={`/search?q=${q}${authorFilter ? `&author=${authorFilter}` : ''}${contentFilter !== 'all' ? `&type=${contentFilter}` : ''}${sortBy !== 'relevance' ? `&sort=${sortBy}` : ''}`}
                 class="text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-200 ml-1"
                 aria-label="Remove work filter">×</a>
            </div>
          )}
          
          <button 
            type="submit" 
            name="type" 
            value="all"
            class:list={[
              "px-3 py-1.5 text-sm rounded-full transition-all",
              contentFilter === "all" 
                ? "bg-cyan-600 text-white" 
                : "bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-600"
            ]}
          >All</button>
          <button 
            type="submit" 
            name="type" 
            value="doha"
            class:list={[
              "px-3 py-1.5 text-sm rounded-full transition-all",
              contentFilter === "doha" 
                ? "bg-cyan-600 text-white" 
                : "bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-600"
            ]}
          >Doha</button>
          <button 
            type="submit" 
            name="type" 
            value="dictionary"
            class:list={[
              "px-3 py-1.5 text-sm rounded-full transition-all",
              contentFilter === "dictionary" 
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white" 
                : "bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-600"
            ]}
          >Dictionary</button>
          <button 
            type="submit" 
            name="type" 
            value="idiom"
            class:list={[
              "px-3 py-1.5 text-sm rounded-full transition-all",
              contentFilter === "idiom" 
                ? "bg-indigo-600 text-white" 
                : "bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-600"
            ]}
          >Idioms</button>
          <button 
            type="submit" 
            name="type" 
            value="article"
            class:list={[
              "px-3 py-1.5 text-sm rounded-full transition-all",
              contentFilter === "article" 
                ? "bg-purple-600 text-white" 
                : "bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-600"
            ]}
          >Articles</button>
          
          <span class="ml-4 text-sm text-slate-400">Sort:</span>
          <button 
            type="submit" 
            name="sort" 
            value="relevance"
            class:list={[
              "px-3 py-1.5 text-sm rounded-full transition-all",
              sortBy === "relevance" 
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white" 
                : "bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-600"
            ]}
          >Relevance</button>
          <button 
            type="submit" 
            name="sort" 
            value="recent"
            class:list={[
              "px-3 py-1.5 text-sm rounded-full transition-all",
              sortBy === "recent" 
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white" 
                : "bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-600"
            ]}
          >Recent</button>
        </div>
      )}
    </form>

    {!q && (
      <div class="text-center py-12 bg-slate-800/30 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
        <div class="text-4xl mb-4">🔍</div>
        <p class="text-slate-400 text-lg mb-2">
          Enter a query to search across all content types
        </p>
        <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-6">
          Try searching by <strong class="text-cyan-400">keyword</strong>, <strong class="text-blue-400">author</strong>, <strong class="text-indigo-400">work</strong>, or <strong class="text-purple-400">phrase</strong>
        </p>
        
        <!-- Search tips -->
        <div class="max-w-2xl mx-auto mb-6 p-4 bg-slate-800/50 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
          <h3 class="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-300 mb-3">Search Tips:</h3>
          <ul class="text-xs text-slate-400 space-y-2 text-left">
            <li class="flex items-start gap-2">
              <span class="text-cyan-400 mt-0.5">•</span>
              <span>Use <strong class="text-slate-300">author filter</strong> to find content by specific authors (e.g., kabir, tulsidas)</span>
            </li>
            <li class="flex items-start gap-2">
              <span class="text-blue-400 mt-0.5">•</span>
              <span>Use <strong class="text-slate-300">work filter</strong> to search within specific literary works</span>
            </li>
            <li class="flex items-start gap-2">
              <span class="text-indigo-400 mt-0.5">•</span>
              <span>Filter by <strong class="text-slate-300">content type</strong> to narrow results to Doha, Dictionary, Idioms, or Articles</span>
            </li>
            <li class="flex items-start gap-2">
              <span class="text-purple-400 mt-0.5">•</span>
              <span>Sort by <strong class="text-slate-300">relevance</strong> (default) or <strong class="text-slate-300">recent</strong></span>
            </li>
          </ul>
        </div>
        
        <div class="mt-6 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3">
          <span class="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-full text-sm text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-2">
            <span class="w-2 h-2 bg-cyan-500 rounded-full"></span> Doha
          </span>
          <span class="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-full text-sm text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-2">
            <span class="w-2 h-2 bg-blue-500 rounded-full"></span> Dictionary
          </span>
          <span class="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-full text-sm text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-2">
            <span class="w-2 h-2 bg-indigo-500 rounded-full"></span> Idioms
          </span>
          <span class="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-full text-sm text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-2">
            <span class="w-2 h-2 bg-purple-500 rounded-full"></span> Articles
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    )}

    {q && (
      <div class="space-y-8">
        <!-- Search summary -->
        <div class="bg-slate-800/50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
          <p class="text-slate-300">
            Found <strong class="text-cyan-400">{totalResults}</strong> result{totalResults !== 1 ? 's' : ''} for <strong class="text-blue-400">"{q}"</strong>
          </p>
          {errors.length > 0 && (
            <p class="text-sm text-orange-400 mt-2">
              Some searches failed: {errors.join(", ")}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        {dohas.length > 0 && (
          <section class="bg-slate-800/50 p-6 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
            <h2 class="text-xl font-bold mb-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 to-blue-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
              Doha ({dohas.length})
            </h2>
            <ul class="space-y-4">
              {dohas.map((d: any) => (
                <li class="border-l-4 border-cyan-400 pl-4 hover:bg-slate-700/30 p-3 rounded transition-colors">
                  <a href={`/doha/${d.id}`} class="text-cyan-300 hover:text-cyan-200 font-medium block mb-1">
                    {d.main_text}
                  </a>
                  <p class="text-sm text-slate-400">{d.meaning}</p>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </section>
        )}

        {dictionary.length > 0 && (
          <section class="bg-slate-800/50 p-6 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
            <h2 class="text-xl font-bold mb-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-400 to-indigo-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
              Dictionary ({dictionary.length})
            </h2>
            <ul class="space-y-4">
              {dictionary.map((w: any) => (
                <li class="border-l-4 border-blue-400 pl-4 hover:bg-slate-700/30 p-3 rounded transition-colors">
                  <a href={`/dictionary/${w.id}`} class="text-blue-300 hover:text-blue-200 font-medium block mb-1">
                    {w.lemma_devanagari}
                  </a>
                  {w.lemma_roman && <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 italic">{w.lemma_roman}</p>}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </section>
        )}

        {idioms.length > 0 && (
          <section class="bg-slate-800/50 p-6 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
            <h2 class="text-xl font-bold mb-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-400 to-purple-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
              Idioms ({idioms.length})
            </h2>
            <ul class="space-y-4">
              {idioms.map((i: any) => (
                <li class="border-l-4 border-indigo-400 pl-4 hover:bg-slate-700/30 p-3 rounded transition-colors">
                  <a href={`/idioms/${i.id}`} class="text-indigo-300 hover:text-indigo-200 font-medium block mb-1">
                    {i.text_devanagari ?? i.text}
                  </a>
                  {i.meaning && <p class="text-sm text-slate-400">{i.meaning.slice(0, 100)}{i.meaning.length > 100 ? '...' : ''}</p>}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </section>
        )}

        {articles.length > 0 && (
          <section class="bg-slate-800/50 p-6 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
            <h2 class="text-xl font-bold mb-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-400 to-pink-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
              Articles ({articles.length})
            </h2>
            <ul class="space-y-4">
              {articles.map((a: any) => (
                <li class="border-l-4 border-purple-400 pl-4 hover:bg-slate-700/30 p-3 rounded transition-colors">
                  <a href={`/articles/${a.id}`} class="text-purple-300 hover:text-purple-200 font-medium block mb-1">
                    {a.title}
                  </a>
                  {a.excerpt && <p class="text-sm text-slate-400">{a.excerpt.slice(0, 150)}{a.excerpt.length > 150 ? '...' : ''}</p>}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </section>
        )}

        {totalResults === 0 && (
          <div class="text-center py-12 bg-slate-800/50 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
            <p class="text-slate-400 text-lg mb-2">
              No results found for <strong class="text-cyan-400">"{q}"</strong>
            </p>
            <p class="text-sm text-slate-500">
              Try different keywords or check your spelling
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    )}
  </div>
</BaseLayout>

=======================================================
src\pages\sitemap.xml.ts
=======================================================
// src/pages/sitemap.xml.ts
import type { APIRoute } from "astro";
import { api } from "../lib/api";

interface SitemapUrl {
  loc: string;
  lastmod?: string;
  changefreq: "always" | "hourly" | "daily" | "weekly" | "monthly" | "yearly" | "never";
  priority: string;
}

export const GET: APIRoute = async () => {
  const baseUrl = (import.meta.env.SITE || "https://awadhi.new").replace(/\/$/, "");
  const urls: SitemapUrl[] = [];
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  try {
    // Static routes with proper priorities
    urls.push(
      { loc: "/", lastmod: now, changefreq: "daily", priority: "1.0" },
      { loc: "/search", lastmod: now, changefreq: "daily", priority: "0.9" },
      { loc: "/doha", lastmod: now, changefreq: "daily", priority: "0.9" },
      { loc: "/dictionary", lastmod: now, changefreq: "daily", priority: "0.9" },
      { loc: "/idioms", lastmod: now, changefreq: "daily", priority: "0.9" },
      { loc: "/articles", lastmod: now, changefreq: "daily", priority: "0.9" },
      { loc: "/submit", lastmod: now, changefreq: "monthly", priority: "0.7" },
    );

    // Dynamic content - Doha entries
    try {
      const dohas = await api("/content/doha?limit=1000&visibility=public");
      const dohaArray = Array.isArray(dohas) ? dohas : dohas?.results || [];
      dohaArray.forEach((d: any) => {
        if (d.id && d.visibility === "public") {
          urls.push({
            loc: `/doha/${d.id}`,
            lastmod: d.updated_at || d.created_at || now,
            changefreq: "weekly",
            priority: "0.8",
          });
        }
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("[Sitemap] Failed to fetch dohas:", e);
    }

    // Dynamic content - Dictionary entries
    try {
      const dictEntries = await api("/dictionary?limit=1000&visibility=public");
      const dictArray = Array.isArray(dictEntries) ? dictEntries : dictEntries?.results || [];
      dictArray.forEach((d: any) => {
        if (d.id && d.visibility === "public") {
          urls.push({
            loc: `/dictionary/${d.id}`,
            lastmod: d.updated_at || d.created_at || now,
            changefreq: "weekly",
            priority: "0.8",
          });
        }
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("[Sitemap] Failed to fetch dictionary entries:", e);
    }

    // Dynamic content - Idioms
    try {
      const idioms = await api("/idioms?limit=1000&visibility=public");
      const idiomsArray = Array.isArray(idioms) ? idioms : idioms?.results || [];
      idiomsArray.forEach((i: any) => {
        if (i.id && i.visibility === "public") {
          urls.push({
            loc: `/idioms/${i.id}`,
            lastmod: i.updated_at || i.created_at || now,
            changefreq: "weekly",
            priority: "0.8",
          });
        }
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("[Sitemap] Failed to fetch idioms:", e);
    }

    // Dynamic content - Articles
    try {
      const articles = await api("/articles?limit=1000&visibility=public");
      const articlesArray = Array.isArray(articles) ? articles : articles?.results || [];
      articlesArray.forEach((a: any) => {
        if (a.id && a.visibility === "public") {
          urls.push({
            loc: `/articles/${a.id}`,
            lastmod: a.updated_at || a.created_at || now,
            changefreq: "weekly",
            priority: "0.8",
          });
        }
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("[Sitemap] Failed to fetch articles:", e);
    }

    // Generate XML with proper formatting
    const xml = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9"
        xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
        xsi:schemaLocation="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9
        http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9/sitemap.xsd">
${urls
  .map(
    (entry) => `  <url>
    <loc>${baseUrl}${entry.loc}</loc>
    ${entry.lastmod ? `<lastmod>${formatDate(entry.lastmod)}</lastmod>` : ""}
    <changefreq>${entry.changefreq}</changefreq>
    <priority>${entry.priority}</priority>
  </url>`
  )
  .join("\n")}
</urlset>`;

    return new Response(xml, {
      status: 200,
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/xml; charset=utf-8",
        "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=3600, s-maxage=7200",
        "X-Robots-Tag": "all",
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[Sitemap] Fatal error:", error);
    
    // Fallback minimal sitemap
    const fallbackXml = `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9">
  <url>
    <loc>${baseUrl}/</loc>
    <lastmod>${now}</lastmod>
    <changefreq>daily</changefreq>
    <priority>1.0</priority>
  </url>
</urlset>`;

    return new Response(fallbackXml, {
      status: 200,
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/xml; charset=utf-8",
      },
    });
  }
};

// Helper function to format dates to W3C format (ISO 8601)
function formatDate(date: string | Date): string {
  try {
    const d = new Date(date);
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) {
      return new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
    }
    return d.toISOString().split("T")[0]; // YYYY-MM-DD format
  } catch {
    return new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
  }
}

export const prerender = false;

import BaseLayout from "../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import { api, ApiError } from "../lib/api";

const { slug } = Astro.params;
const segments = Array.isArray(slug) ? slug : [slug || ""];
const n = segments.length;

// top-level content namespaces that map to canonical content/by-path
const CONTENT_NAMESPACES = new Set(["dictionary", "idioms", "articles", "doha", "content"]);

const joinPath = (parts: string[]) => parts.filter(Boolean).join("/");

let data: any = null;
let error: string | null = null;
let pageTitle = "Awadhi New";
let canonicalUrl = `https://awadhi.new/${joinPath(segments)}`;

try {
  if (n === 0 || (n === 1 && segments[0] === "")) {
    throw new ApiError(404, "Not found");
  }

  const first = segments[0];

  // 1) If the URL starts with a top-level content namespace, use content/by-path
  if (CONTENT_NAMESPACES.has(first) && n >= 2) {
    const hierarchyPath = joinPath(segments);
    data = await api(`/content/by-path/${hierarchyPath}`);
    const titleCandidate = data?.title || data?.main_text || data?.text_devanagari || segments.join(" · ");
    pageTitle = `${titleCandidate} · Awadhi New`;
    canonicalUrl = `https://awadhi.new/${hierarchyPath}`;
  } else if (n === 1) {
    // Author page
    const authorSlug = segments[0];
    const author: any = await api(`/authors/${authorSlug}`);
    const works = await api(`/authors/${authorSlug}/works`);
    const dohas: any = await api(`/search?author=${authorSlug}`);
    data = { ...author, __works: works, __dohas: dohas.results };
    pageTitle = `${data?.name ?? authorSlug} · Awadhi New`;
  } else if (n === 2) {
    // Works list for author
    const [authorSlug] = segments;
    data = await api(`/authors/${authorSlug}/works`);
    pageTitle = `${authorSlug} · Works · Awadhi New`;
  } else if (n === 3) {
    // Chapters list for work
    const [authorSlug, workSlug] = segments;
    data = await api(`/authors/${authorSlug}/works/${workSlug}/chapters`);
    pageTitle = `${workSlug} · Chapters · Awadhi New`;
  } else {
    // Fallback: 4+ segments — treat as canonical path
    const hierarchyPath = joinPath(segments);
    data = await api(`/content/by-path/${hierarchyPath}`);
    const titleCandidate = data?.title || data?.main_text || data?.text_devanagari || segments.join(" · ");
    pageTitle = `${titleCandidate} · Awadhi New`;
    canonicalUrl = `https://awadhi.new/${hierarchyPath}`;
  }
} catch (e) {
  if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404) {
    return Astro.redirect("/404");
  }
  error = "Content unavailable at the moment.";
  if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
    console.error("content route error:", e);
  }
}

type DohaSearchResult = {
  id: number;
  main_text: string;
};

const authorDohas: DohaSearchResult[] =
  !error && data && n === 1 && Array.isArray(data.__dohas)
    ? (data.__dohas as DohaSearchResult[])
    : [];
---

<BaseLayout title={pageTitle}>
  <head>
    <link rel="canonical" href={canonicalUrl} />
  </head>

  {error && <p class="text-red-600">{error}</p>}

  {/* Author page */}
  {!error && data && n === 1 && (
    <section>
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-serif">{data.name}</h1>
      {data.short_bio && (
        <p class="text-sm text-stone-600">{data.short_bio}</p>
      )}

      <h2 class="mt-6 font-semibold">Works</h2>

      {data.__works && data.__works.length > 0 ? (
        <ul class="space-y-3 mt-2">
          {data.__works.map((w: any) => (
            <li>
              <a
                href={`/${data.slug}/${w.slug}`}
                class="underline"
              >
                {w.title}
              </a>
              {w.description && (
                <p class="text-sm text-stone-600">{w.description}</p>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : (
        <p class="text-sm text-stone-600">No works listed.</p>
      )}

      <h2 class="mt-8 font-semibold">Dohas</h2>

      {authorDohas.length ? (
        <ul class="space-y-2 mt-2">
          {authorDohas.map((d) => (
            <li>
              <a href={`/doha/${d.id}`} class="underline">
                {d.main_text.split("॥")[0]}॥
              </a>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : (
        <p class="text-sm text-stone-600">No dohas found.</p>
      )}
    </section>
  )}

  {/* Works list (author) */}
  {!error && data && n === 2 && (
    <section>
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-serif">Works</h1>
      {Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0 ? (
        <ul class="space-y-3 mt-2">
          {data.map((w: any) => (
            <li>
              <a href={`/${segments[0]}/${w.slug}`} class="underline">{w.title}</a>
              {w.description && <p class="text-sm text-stone-600">{w.description}</p>}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : (
        <p class="text-sm text-stone-600">No works found for this author.</p>
      )}
    </section>
  )}

  {/* Chapters list */}
  {!error && data && n === 3 && (
    <section>
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-serif">Chapters</h1>
      {Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0 ? (
        <ul class="space-y-3 mt-2">
          {data.map((c: any) => (
            <li>
              <a href={`/${segments[0]}/${segments[1]}/${c.slug}`} class="underline">
                {c.title || c.slug}
              </a>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : (
        <p class="text-sm text-stone-600">No chapters found for this work.</p>
      )}
    </section>
  )}

  {/* Canonical content (dictionary/idiom/article/doha or deep path) */}
  {!error && data && (n >= 2 || n >= 4) && (
    <article class="space-y-6">
      <header>
        <h1 class="text-2xl font-serif">
          {data.text_devanagari ?? data.title ?? data.main_text ?? segments.join(" · ")}
        </h1>

        {data.text_romanized && <p class="italic text-stone-600">{data.text_romanized}</p>}
      </header>

      {data.meaning && (
        <section>
          <h2 class="font-semibold mb-1">Meaning</h2>
          <p>{data.meaning}</p>
        </section>
      )}

      <footer class="text-sm text-stone-500">
        {typeof data.version !== "undefined" && <p>Version: {data.version}</p>}
        {data.status && <p>Status: {data.status}</p>}
      </footer>
    </article>
  )}
</BaseLayout>

src\pages\articles\[id].astro

---
export const prerender = false;

import BaseLayout from "../../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import { api, ApiError } from "../../lib/api";
import Recommendations from "../../components/recommendations/Recommendations.svelte";
import InteractionBar from "../../components/interaction/InteractionBar.svelte";
import CopyButton from "../../components/content/CopyButton.svelte";
import StructuredData from "../../components/StructuredData.astro";
import TrustSignals from "../../components/content/TrustSignals.svelte";
import ModerationInfo from "../../components/content/ModerationInfo.svelte";

const { id } = Astro.params;

let article: any = null;

try {
  article = await api(`/articles/${id}`);
} catch (e) {
  if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404) {
    return Astro.redirect("/404");
  }
  if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
    console.error("[Article Detail] Error:", e);
  }
  throw e;
}

const title = `${article.title} — Awadhi New`;
const description =
  article.body
    ? article.body.slice(0, 160)
    : `Article: ${article.title}`;

const baseUrl = import.meta.env.SITE || "https://awadhi.new";
const articleUrl = `${baseUrl}/articles/${id}`;
---

<BaseLayout title={title}>
  <head>
    <link rel="canonical" href={articleUrl} />
    <meta name="description" content={description} />
    
    <!-- Structured Data for Article -->
    <StructuredData 
      type="Article" 
      data={{
        title: article.title,
        description: description,
        excerpt: article.body?.slice(0, 160),
        datePublished: article.created_at,
        dateModified: article.updated_at || article.created_at,
        url: articleUrl,
        author: article.author_name
      }} 
    />
    
    <!-- Breadcrumb Structured Data -->
    <StructuredData 
      type="BreadcrumbList"
      data={{
        items: [
          { name: "Home", url: baseUrl },
          { name: "Articles", url: `${baseUrl}/articles` },
          { name: article.title, url: articleUrl }
        ]
      }}
    />
  </head>

  <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">
    <!-- Breadcrumbs -->
    <nav class="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-6">
      <a href="/" class="hover:text-cyan-400 transition-colors">Home</a>
      <span class="mx-2">›</span>
      <a href="/articles" class="hover:text-purple-400 transition-colors">Articles</a>
      <span class="mx-2">›</span>
      <span class="text-slate-400">{article.title}</span>
    </nav>

    <article class="space-y-8">
      <!-- Trust Signals -->
      <div class="flex justify-center">
        <TrustSignals 
          client:load
          isCanonical={article.is_canonical ?? false}
          confidenceLevel={article.confidence_level}
          sourceReference={article.source_reference}
          verifiedBy={article.verified_by}
          verifiedAt={article.verified_at}
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <h1 class="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-serif text-center text-slate-100 mb-4">{article.title}</h1>
        <!-- Copy button -->
        <div class="flex justify-center">
          <CopyButton text={article.title} label="Title" color="purple" client:load />
        </div>
      </div>

      <section class="prose prose-invert prose-lg max-w-none bg-slate-800/50 p-8 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
        <p class="text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">{article.body}</p>
      </section>

      <!-- Moderation Info -->
      <ModerationInfo 
        client:load
        createdAt={article.created_at}
        updatedAt={article.updated_at}
        version={article.version || 1}
        hasVersionHistory={article.version > 1}
        contentType="article"
        contentId={article.id}
      />

      <!-- Interaction buttons: likes, bookmarks, shares, reports -->
      <div class="py-6 border-t border-b border-slate-700 flex justify-center">
        <InteractionBar
          client:only="svelte"
          contentType="article"
          contentId={article.id}
          likes={article?.likes_count ?? 0}
          bookmarks={article?.bookmarks_count ?? 0}
          shares={article?.shares_count ?? 0}
        />
      </div>

    <Recommendations content_type="article" content_id={article.id} client:load />

    <div class="text-center pt-4">
      <a href="/articles" class="text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 underline">← Back to Articles</a>
    </div>
  </article>
  </div>
</BaseLayout>

// Demo page to test error handling - DEVELOPMENT ONLY
import BaseLayout from "../../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";

export const prerender = false;

// Only allow in development
if (!import.meta.env.DEV) {
  return Astro.redirect("/");
}
---

<BaseLayout title="Error Handling Demo · Dev Only" noindex>
  <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto">
    <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-400 to-pink-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
      Error Handling Demo
    </h1>
    
    <div class="space-y-6">
      <!-- Error Pages -->
      <section class="p-6 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg">
        <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-cyan-400 mb-4">Error Pages</h2>
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          <a href="/404" class="px-4 py-3 bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 border border-slate-600 text-slate-200 rounded text-center transition-colors">
            404 - Not Found
          </a>
          <a href="/403" class="px-4 py-3 bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 border border-slate-600 text-slate-200 rounded text-center transition-colors">
            403 - Access Denied
          </a>
          <a href="/500" class="px-4 py-3 bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 border border-slate-600 text-slate-200 rounded text-center transition-colors">
            500 - Server Error
          </a>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- Component Demos -->
      <section class="p-6 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg">
        <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-indigo-400 mb-4">Error Components</h2>
        <div id="demo-container"></div>
      </section>

      <!-- Documentation -->
      <section class="p-6 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg">
        <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-pink-400 mb-4">Documentation</h2>
        <p class="text-slate-400 mb-4">
          See <code class="px-2 py-1 bg-slate-900 rounded text-pink-300">ERROR_HANDLING_GUIDE.md</code> for complete documentation.
        </p>
        <ul class="list-disc list-inside space-y-2 text-slate-400">
          <li>ErrorBoundary - Global error catching (auto-integrated)</li>
          <li>RateLimitFeedback - Rate limit toast notifications</li>
          <li>ErrorDisplay - Inline error messages</li>
          <li>errorHandler.ts - Utility functions for error parsing</li>
        </ul>
      </section>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    import ErrorDisplay from "../components/ErrorDisplay.svelte";
    import RateLimitFeedback from "../components/RateLimitFeedback.svelte";

    // Demo: ErrorDisplay
    const container = document.getElementById("demo-container");
    if (container) {
      const errorDisplay = new ErrorDisplay({
        target: container,
        props: {
          error: "This is a demo error message",
          type: "error",
          title: "Demo Error",
          dismissible: true,
          action: {
            label: "Retry",
            onClick: () => alert("Retry clicked!")
          }
        }
      });

      // Demo: Rate Limit Feedback
      setTimeout(() => {
        const rateLimitDemo = new RateLimitFeedback({
          target: document.body,
          props: {
            show: true,
            retryAfter: 10,
            message: "Demo rate limit - wait 10 seconds"
          }
        });
      }, 2000);
    }
  </script>
</BaseLayout>

import BaseLayout from "../../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";

let entry: any = null;

try {
  entry = await api(`/dictionary/${id}`);
} catch (e) {
  if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404) {
    return Astro.redirect("/404");
  }
  if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
    console.error("[Dictionary Detail] Error:", e);
  }
  throw e;
}

const title = `${entry.lemma_devanagari} — Awadhi Dictionary`;
const description =
  entry.meaning
    ? entry.meaning.slice(0, 160)
    : `Meaning and usage of ${entry.lemma_devanagari}`;

    <!-- Structured Data for Dictionary Entry -->
    <StructuredData 
      type="DefinedTerm" 
      data={{
        term: entry.lemma_devanagari,
        definition: entry.meaning,
        termCode: entry.lemma_roman
      }} 
    />
    
    <!-- Breadcrumb Structured Data -->
    <StructuredData 
      type="BreadcrumbList"
      data={{
        items: [
          { name: "Home", url: baseUrl },
          { name: "Dictionary", url: `${baseUrl}/dictionary` },
          { name: entry.lemma_devanagari, url: entryUrl }
        ]
      }}
    />
  </head>

      <a href="/" class="hover:text-cyan-400 transition-colors">Home</a>
      <span class="mx-2">›</span>
      <a href="/dictionary" class="hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors">Dictionary</a>
      <span class="mx-2">›</span>
      <span class="text-slate-400">{entry.lemma_devanagari}</span>
    </nav>

          sourceReference={entry.source_reference}
          verifiedBy={entry.verified_by}
          verifiedAt={entry.verified_at}
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <h1 class="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-serif text-center text-slate-100 mb-4">{entry.lemma_devanagari}</h1>
        <!-- Copy button -->
        <div class="flex justify-center">
          <CopyButton text={entry.lemma_devanagari} label="Word" color="blue" client:load />
        </div>
      </div>

      {entry.lemma_roman && (
        <p class="italic text-slate-400 text-center text-lg">{entry.lemma_roman}</p>
      )}

      {entry.meaning && (
        <section class="bg-slate-800/50 p-6 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
          <h2 class="font-semibold text-cyan-400 mb-3">Meaning</h2>
          <p class="text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">{entry.meaning}</p>
        </section>
      )}

      <footer class="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center pt-4">
        <p>Language: {entry.language}</p>
        <p>Version: {entry.version}</p>
      </footer>

      <!-- Interaction buttons: likes, bookmarks, shares, reports -->
      <div class="py-6 border-t border-b border-slate-700 flex justify-center">
        <InteractionBar
          client:only="svelte"
          contentType="dictionary"
          contentId={entry.id}
          likes={entry?.likes_count ?? 0}
          bookmarks={entry?.bookmarks_count ?? 0}
          shares={entry?.shares_count ?? 0}
        />
      </div>

    <Recommendations content_type="dictionary" content_id={entry.id} client:load />

    <div class="text-center pt-4">
      <a href="/dictionary" class="text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 underline">← Back to Dictionary</a>
    </div>

          { name: `#${doha.id}`, url: dohaUrl }
        ]
      }}
    />
  </head>
  
  <div class="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">
    <!-- Breadcrumbs -->
    <nav class="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-6">
      <a href="/" class="hover:text-cyan-400 transition-colors">Home</a>
      <span class="mx-2">›</span>
      <a href="/doha" class="hover:text-cyan-400 transition-colors">Doha</a>
      <span class="mx-2">›</span>
      <span class="text-slate-400">#{doha.id}</span>
    </nav>

    <article class="space-y-8">
      <!-- Trust Signals -->
      <div class="flex justify-center">
        <TrustSignals 
          client:load
          isCanonical={doha.is_canonical ?? false}
          confidenceLevel={doha.confidence_level}
          sourceReference={doha.source_reference}
          verifiedBy={doha.verified_by}
          verifiedAt={doha.verified_at}
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <h1 class="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-serif leading-relaxed text-center text-slate-100 mb-4">
          {doha.main_text}
        </h1>
        <!-- Copy button -->
        <div class="flex justify-center">
          <CopyButton text={doha.main_text} label="Text" color="cyan" client:load />
        </div>
      </div>

      <p class="text-lg text-slate-300 leading-relaxed text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto">{doha.meaning}</p>

      <!-- Moderation Info -->
      <ModerationInfo 
        client:load
        createdAt={doha.created_at}
        updatedAt={doha.updated_at}
        version={doha.version || 1}
        hasVersionHistory={doha.version > 1}
        contentType="doha"
        contentId={doha.id}
      />

      <!-- Interaction buttons: likes, bookmarks, shares, reports -->
      <div class="py-6 border-t border-b border-slate-700 flex justify-center">
        <InteractionBar
          client:only="svelte"
          contentType="doha"
          contentId={doha.id}
          likes={doha?.likes_count ?? 0}
          bookmarks={doha?.bookmarks_count ?? 0}
          shares={doha?.shares_count ?? 0}
        />
      </div>

    <!-- ✅ ALWAYS RENDER -->
    <Recommendations
      content_type="doha"
      content_id={doha.id}
      client:load
    />

    <!-- Content history (Doha only) -->
    <ContentHistory dohaId={doha.id} client:load />

    <div class="text-center pt-4">
      <a href="/doha" class="text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 underline">← Back to Doha</a>
    </div>
  </article>
  </div>

export const prerender = false;

import BaseLayout from "../../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import { api, ApiError } from "../../lib/api";
import Recommendations from "../../components/recommendations/Recommendations.svelte";
import InteractionBar from "../../components/interaction/InteractionBar.svelte";

import StructuredData from "../../components/StructuredData.astro";
import TrustSignals from "../../components/content/TrustSignals.svelte";
import ModerationInfo from "../../components/content/ModerationInfo.svelte";

const { id } = Astro.params;

let idiom: any = null;
let error: string | null = null;

try {
  idiom = await api(`/idioms/${id}`);
} catch (e) {
  if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404) {
    return Astro.redirect("/404");
  }
  error = "Unable to load the idiom.";
  if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.error("[Idiom Detail] Error:", e);
}

const title = `${idiom?.text_devanagari ?? idiom?.text ?? `Idiom ${id}`} — Awadhi Idiom`;
const description =
  idiom?.meaning
    ? String(idiom.meaning).slice(0, 160)
    : `Meaning and usage of the idiom ${idiom?.text_devanagari ?? idiom?.text ?? id}`;

const baseUrl = import.meta.env.SITE || "https://awadhi.new";
const idiomUrl = `${baseUrl}/idioms/${id}`;
---

<BaseLayout title={title}>
  <head>
    <link rel="canonical" href={idiomUrl} />
    <meta name="description" content={description} />
    
    <!-- Structured Data for Idiom (Creative Work) -->
    <StructuredData 
      type="CreativeWork" 
      data={{
        text: idiom?.text_devanagari || idiom?.text,
        meaning: idiom?.meaning,
        description: description,
        datePublished: idiom?.created_at
      }} 
    />
    
    <!-- Breadcrumb Structured Data -->
    <StructuredData 
      type="BreadcrumbList"
      data={{
        items: [
          { name: "Home", url: baseUrl },
          { name: "Idioms", url: `${baseUrl}/idioms` },
          { name: `#${id}`, url: idiomUrl }
        ]
      }}
    />
  </head>

  <div class="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">
    {error && <p class="text-red-400 text-center">{error}</p>}

    {!error && idiom && (
      <>
        <!-- Breadcrumbs -->
        <nav class="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-6">
          <a href="/" class="hover:text-cyan-400 transition-colors">Home</a>
          <span class="mx-2">›</span>
          <a href="/idioms" class="hover:text-indigo-400 transition-colors">Idioms</a>
          <span class="mx-2">›</span>
          <span class="text-slate-400">#{idiom.id}</span>
        </nav>

        <article class="space-y-8">
          <!-- Trust Signals -->
          <div class="flex justify-center">
            <TrustSignals 
              client:load
              isCanonical={idiom.is_canonical ?? false}
              confidenceLevel={idiom.confidence_level}
              sourceReference={idiom.source_reference}
              verifiedBy={idiom.verified_by}
              verifiedAt={idiom.verified_at}
            />
          </div>

            <!-- Copy button -->

          {idiom.meaning && (
            <section class="bg-slate-800/50 p-6 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">

          {idiom.usage && (

            </section>

            updatedAt={idiom.updated_at}
            version={idiom.version || 1}
            hasVersionHistory={idiom.version > 1}
            contentType="idiom"
            contentId={idiom.id}
          />

          <footer class="text-sm text-slate-500 text-center pt-4">
            {typeof idiom.version !== "undefined" && <p>Version: {idiom.version}</p>}
          </footer>

          {/* Interaction buttons: likes, bookmarks, shares, reports */}
          <div class="py-6 border-t border-b border-slate-700 flex justify-center">
            <InteractionBar
              client:only="svelte"
              contentType="idiom"
              contentId={idiom.id}
              likes={idiom?.likes_count ?? 0}
              bookmarks={idiom?.bookmarks_count ?? 0}
              shares={idiom?.shares_count ?? 0}
            />

            <a href="/idioms" class="text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 underline">← Back to Idioms</a>

// src/pages/submissions/[id].astro
export const prerender = false;

import BaseLayout from "../../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import { api, ApiError } from "../../lib/api";

const { id } = Astro.params;

let submission: any = null;
let error: string | null = null;

try {
  submission = await api(`/submissions/${id}`);
} catch (e) {
  if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404) {
    return Astro.redirect("/404");
  }
  error = "Unable to load submission.";
  if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.error("[Submission Detail] Error:", e);
}

const title = submission
  ? `Submission #${id} · ${submission.content_type ?? "Content"}`
  : `Submission · Awadhi New`;
---

<BaseLayout title={title}>
  <head>
    <meta name="robots" content="noindex,nofollow" />
  </head>

  <nav class="text-sm text-stone-600 mb-4">
    <a href="/">Home</a> › <a href="/submissions">My Submissions</a> › #{id}
  </nav>

  {error && <p class="text-red-600">{error}</p>}

  {!error && submission && (
    <article class="space-y-6 max-w-3xl">
      <div class="flex items-start justify-between">
        <h1 class="text-3xl font-serif">
          {submission.content_type ?? "Submission"} #{id}
        </h1>
        <div class="text-sm">
          <span class={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full font-medium ${
            submission.status === 'approved' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' :
            submission.status === 'rejected' ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-700' :
            submission.status === 'pending' ? 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700' :
            'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700'
          }`}>
            {submission.status}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <section class="bg-stone-50 p-4 rounded border">
        <h2 class="font-semibold mb-2">Content</h2>
        {submission.main_text && (
          <div class="mb-3">
            <div class="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600">Main Text:</div>
            <div class="text-lg">{submission.main_text}</div>
          </div>
        )}
        {submission.meaning && (
          <div class="mb-3">
            <div class="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600">Meaning:</div>
            <div>{submission.meaning}</div>
          </div>
        )}
        {submission.metadata && typeof submission.metadata === 'object' && (
          <div class="mb-3">
            <div class="text-sm font-medium text-stone-600">Metadata:</div>
            <pre class="text-xs bg-white p-2 rounded border overflow-auto">{JSON.stringify(submission.metadata, null, 2)}</pre>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      <section class="text-sm text-stone-600">
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <div>
            <strong>Visibility:</strong> {submission.visibility ?? "draft"}
          </div>
          <div>
            <strong>Version:</strong> {submission.version ?? 1}
          </div>
          <div>
            <strong>Submitted:</strong> {submission.created_at ? new Date(submission.created_at).toLocaleString() : "—"}
          </div>
          {submission.updated_at && (
            <div>
              <strong>Updated:</strong> {new Date(submission.updated_at).toLocaleString()}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {submission.moderator_notes && (
        <section class="bg-blue-50 p-4 rounded border border-blue-200">
          <h2 class="font-semibold text-blue-900 mb-2">Moderator Notes</h2>
          <p class="text-sm">{submission.moderator_notes}</p>
        </section>
      )}

      <div class="flex gap-3 pt-4 border-t">
        <a href="/submissions" class="px-4 py-2 rounded border hover:bg-stone-50">← Back to My Submissions</a>
        {submission.status === 'draft' && (
          <a href={`/submit?edit=${id}`} class="px-4 py-2 rounded bg-blue-600 text-white hover:bg-blue-700">
            Edit Submission
          </a>
        )}
      </div>
    </article>
  )}
</BaseLayout>

// src/pages/users/[username].astro
// Public user profile - SEO-indexable
export const prerender = false;

import BaseLayout from "../../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import { api, ApiError } from "../../lib/api";
import UserStats from "../../components/user/UserStats.svelte";

const { username } = Astro.params;

// Check if viewing own profile
const currentUser = Astro.locals.user; // From auth middleware
const isOwnProfile = currentUser && currentUser.username === username;

let user: any = null;
let error: string | null = null;

try {
  user = await api(`/users/${username}`);
} catch (e) {
  if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404) {
    error = "User not found";
  } else {
    error = "Failed to load user profile";
  }
  if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
    console.error("[User Profile] Error:", e);
  }
}

// Dummy stats implementation (TODO: Backend API needed)
// GitHub Issue: Add GET /users/:username/stats endpoint
// Should return: { public_submissions: number, approved_count: number, likes_received: number }
const dummyStats = {
  public_submissions: 0,  // Count of approved public submissions
  approved_count: 0,      // Count of approved content
  likes_received: 0       // Total likes on user's approved content
};

// SEO metadata
const title = user ? `${user.username} · Awadhi New` : `User ${username} · Awadhi New`;
const description = user 
  ? `View ${user.username}'s public profile on Awadhi New. Role: ${user.role}. Member since ${new Date(user.created_at).getFullYear()}.`
  : `User profile for ${username}`;
---

<BaseLayout title={title}>
  <head>
    <!-- ✅ SEO-indexable: Public profiles are crawlable -->
    <link rel="canonical" href={`https://awadhi.new/users/${username}`} />
    <meta name="description" content={description} />
  </head>

  <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">
    {error ? (
      <div class="text-center py-12">
        <div class="bg-red-950 border border-red-800 rounded-lg p-8 inline-block">
          <p class="text-red-300 text-lg font-semibold mb-4">{error}</p>
          <a href="/authors" class="text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 underline">
            Browse Authors
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    ) : user && (
      <article class="space-y-8">
        <!-- User Header -->
        <div class="bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-800 via-slate-800 to-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg p-8 text-center">
          <div class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-20 h-20 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500 to-blue-500 mb-4">
            <span class="text-3xl font-bold text-white">
              {user.username.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
            </span>
          </div>
          
          <h1 class="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-serif font-bold mb-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 via-blue-400 to-indigo-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
            {user.username}
          </h1>
          
          <div class="flex justify-center items-center gap-2 mb-4">
            <span 
              class="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-semibold text-sm uppercase tracking-wide"
              class:list={[
                user.role === 'admin' && 'bg-red-900/50 text-red-200 border border-red-700',
                user.role === 'moderator' && 'bg-purple-900/50 text-purple-200 border border-purple-700',
                user.role === 'contributor' && 'bg-blue-900/50 text-blue-200 border border-blue-700',
                user.role === 'registered' && 'bg-slate-700/50 text-slate-200 border border-slate-600'
              ]}
            >
              {user.role}

                {new Date(user.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { 
                  year: 'numeric', 
                  month: 'long'
                })}
              </time>
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <!-- Public Statistics -->
        <div class="bg-slate-800/50 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg p-6">
          <h2 class="text-xl font-semibold text-cyan-400 mb-6 flex items-center gap-2">

              <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 19v-6a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2zm0 0V9a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v10m-6 0a2 2 0 002 2h2a2 2 0 002-2m0 0V5a2 2 0 012-2h2a2 2 0 012 2v14a2 2 0 01-2 2h-2a2 2 0 01-2-2z"></path>
            </svg>

src\pages\[author]\[work].astro

export const prerender = false;
import BaseLayout from "../../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import { api } from "../../lib/api";

const { author, work } = Astro.params;

const safeDecode = (s: any) => {
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(s);
  } catch {
    return s;
  }
};

let chapters = [];
try {
  chapters = await api(`/authors/${author}/works/${work}/chapters`);
} catch {
  chapters = [];
}
---

<BaseLayout title={`${safeDecode(work)} · Awadhi New`}>
  <nav class="text-sm text-stone-500 mb-4">
    <a href="/">Home</a> ›
    <a href={`/${author}`}>{safeDecode(author)}</a> ›
    <span>{safeDecode(work)}</span>
  </nav>

  <h1 class="text-2xl font-serif mb-6">
    {safeDecode(work).replace(/-/g, " ")}
  </h1>

  {
    chapters.length === 0
      ? (
        <p class="text-stone-600">
          No chapters available for this work yet.
          <br />
          <a href={`/${author}`} class="underline">← Back to author</a>
        </p>
      )
      : (
        <ul class="space-y-3">
          {(chapters as any[]).map((c) => (
            <li>
              <a
                href={`/${author}/${work}/${c.slug}`}
                class="underline"
              >
                {c.title || c.slug}
              </a>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )
  }
</BaseLayout>

=======================================================
src\pages\[author]\[work]\[chapter].astro
=======================================================
---
export const prerender = false;

import BaseLayout from "../../../layouts/BaseLayout.astro";
import { api } from "../../../lib/api";

/**
 * Chapter page:
 * - Leaf attempt: /content/by-path/{hierarchy_path}  (hierarchy encoded as ONE path param)
 * - Container fallback: /search?author=...&work=...&chapter=...
 * - If both empty -> show friendly "no content yet" (do NOT redirect to 404)
 */

const params = Astro.params ?? {};
const author = params.author ?? null;
const work = params.work ?? null;
const chapter = params.chapter ?? null;

// Basic param validation
if (!author || !work || !chapter) {
  if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.error("[Chapter] Missing params:", { author, work, chapter });
  return Astro.redirect("/404");
}

const safeDecode = (s) => {
  try { return s ? decodeURIComponent(String(s)) : ""; }
  catch (e) { return String(s ?? ""); }
};
const displayTitle = (() => (safeDecode(chapter) || "").replace(/-/g, " "));

const hierarchy = `${author}/${work}/${chapter}`; // full hierarchy, will be encoded into a single param
const leafPath = `/content/by-path/${encodeURIComponent(hierarchy)}`;
const containerQuery = `/search?author=${encodeURIComponent(author)}&work=${encodeURIComponent(work)}&chapter=${encodeURIComponent(chapter)}`;

let leafContent = null;
let containerItems = [];
let pageError = null;
let isEmptyChapter = false;

async function tryFetchLeaf() {
  try {
    const res = await api(leafPath);
    // treat as leaf only if it has expected fields
    if (res && typeof res === "object" && (res.main_text || res.title || res.id || res.hierarchy_path)) {
      return res;
    }
    return null;
  } catch (e) {
    // e is ApiError object with .status most likely
    if (e && typeof e === "object" && "status" in e) {
      // bubble status up
      throw e;
    }
    throw e;
  }
}

async function tryFetchContainer() {
  try {
    const res = await api(containerQuery);
    // Accept multiple shapes: array or { results: [...] }
    if (Array.isArray(res)) return res;
    if (res && Array.isArray(res.results)) return res.results;
    // Some responses might be object with `items` — handle defensively
    if (res && Array.isArray(res.items)) return res.items;
    return [];
  } catch (e) {
    throw e;
  }
}

// Server-side fetch flow
try {
  // 1) Try leaf
  try {
    const leaf = await tryFetchLeaf();
    if (leaf) leafContent = leaf;
  } catch (leafErr) {
    if (leafErr && leafErr.status === 404) {
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.debug("[Chapter] Leaf not found (404), will try container", leafPath);
    } else {
      console.error("[Chapter] Leaf fetch error (non-404):", leafErr, { leafPath });
      pageError = "Unable to load chapter content at the moment.";
    }
  }

  // 2) If no leaf AND no fatal error, try container (search)
  if (!leafContent && !pageError) {
    try {
      const items = await tryFetchContainer();
      containerItems = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
      // Filter to safe items only (id or main_text/title)
      containerItems = containerItems.filter((it) => it && (it.id || it.main_text || it.title || it.hierarchy_path));
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.debug("[Chapter] containerItems", containerItems.length, { containerQuery });

      // If both leaf & container empty -> show friendly empty state (do not redirect)
      if (!leafContent && containerItems.length === 0) {
        isEmptyChapter = true;
      }
    } catch (containerErr) {
      if (containerErr && containerErr.status === 404) {
        // Backend doesn't provide container -> treat as empty
        if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.debug("[Chapter] container not found (404)", containerQuery);
        isEmptyChapter = true;
      } else {
        console.error("[Chapter] Container fetch error:", containerErr, { containerQuery });
        pageError = "Unable to load chapter items at the moment.";
      }
    }
  }
} catch (err) {
  console.error("[Chapter] Unexpected error:", err);
  pageError = "Unexpected error while loading chapter.";
}
---

<BaseLayout title={displayTitle || "Chapter · Awadhi New"}>
  <nav class="text-sm text-stone-500 mb-4">
    <a href="/">Home</a> ›
    <a href={`/${encodeURIComponent(author)}`}>{safeDecode(author)}</a> ›
    <a href={`/${encodeURIComponent(author)}/${encodeURIComponent(work)}`}>{safeDecode(work).replace(/-/g," ")}</a> ›
    <span>{displayTitle}</span>
  </nav>

  {pageError && (
    <div class="bg-yellow-50 border border-yellow-200 p-4 rounded mb-4">
      <p class="text-sm text-yellow-800">{pageError}</p>
    </div>
  )}

  {leafContent && (
    <article class="space-y-6">
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-serif leading-relaxed">{leafContent.title ?? leafContent.main_text ?? displayTitle}</h1>

      {leafContent.main_text && (
        <div class="prose">
          <p>{leafContent.main_text}</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {leafContent.meaning && (
        <section>
          <h2 class="font-semibold mt-4">Meaning</h2>
          <p class="text-stone-700">{leafContent.meaning}</p>
        </section>
      )}

      <footer class="text-sm text-stone-500 mt-6">
        {leafContent.version && <div>Version: {leafContent.version}</div>}
        <a href={`/${encodeURIComponent(author)}/${encodeURIComponent(work)}`} class="underline">← Back to {safeDecode(work).replace(/-/g," ")}</a>
      </footer>
    </article>
  )}

  {!leafContent && isEmptyChapter && (
    <div class="bg-white border border-stone-200 p-6 rounded">
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-serif mb-3">{displayTitle}</h1>
      <p class="text-stone-600 mb-4">Oops — there is no content in this chapter yet. You can check other chapters or return to the work page.</p>
      <div class="flex gap-4">
        <a href={`/${encodeURIComponent(author)}/${encodeURIComponent(work)}`} class="underline">Back to {safeDecode(work).replace(/-/g," ")}</a>
        <a href={`/${encodeURIComponent(author)}`} class="underline">All works by {safeDecode(author)}</a>
      </div>
    </div>
  )}

  {!leafContent && containerItems.length > 0 && (
    <section>
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-serif mb-4">{displayTitle}</h1>
      <ul class="space-y-3 list-inside">
        {containerItems.map((i) => {
          const itemId = i && (i.id ?? i.pk ?? i._id);
          const itemLabel = i && (i.title || i.main_text || i.hierarchy_path || `Item ${itemId}`);
          return (
            <li>
              {itemId ? (
                <a href={`/doha/${encodeURIComponent(itemId)}`} class="underline">
                  {String(itemLabel).slice(0, 180)}
                </a>
              ) : (
                <span class="text-stone-600">{String(itemLabel).slice(0, 180)}</span>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>

      <div class="mt-6">
        <a href={`/${encodeURIComponent(author)}/${encodeURIComponent(work)}`} class="underline">← Back to {safeDecode(work).replace(/-/g, " ")}</a>
      </div>
    </section>
  )}
</BaseLayout>
